  txFiltered.forEach(function(t) {
    var amt = Number(t.amount) || 0;
    if (amt < 0) revenus  += Math.abs(amt);
    else          depenses += amt;
  });

  // Somme directe (positif = dépôt épargne, négatif = retrait) — même logique que GAS
  var savingsFiltered = txFiltered.reduce(function(s, t) {
    return savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id)) ? s + (Number(t.amount) || 0) : s;
  }, 0);

  // ── KPIs dépenses / budget : TOUJOURS sur le mois en cours ─────────────────
  var _curPeriod = _dbCurrentPeriod();
  var txCurMonth = txAll.filter(function(t) {
    return _normalizePeriodKey(t.period) === _curPeriod;
  });
  // sortiesKpi  = tout amount > 0 hors épargne (dépenses réelles)
  // entreesKpi  = tout amount < 0 hors épargne (revenus + remboursements)
  // econNetKpi  = net épargne ce mois (dépôts - retraits, convention WIMM : dépôt = amount positif)
  var sortiesKpi = 0, entreesKpi = 0, econNetKpi = 0;
  var _savIds = _dbGetSavingsIds();
  txCurMonth.forEach(function(t) {
    var amt = Number(t.amount) || 0;
    if (amt === 0) return;
    var isSaving = _savIds.has(String(t.category_id));
    if (isSaving) {
      econNetKpi += amt; // somme directe : dépôt positif, retrait négatif (convention WIMM)
    } else {
      if (amt > 0) sortiesKpi += amt;
      else         entreesKpi += Math.abs(amt);
    }
  });
  // Total net = sorties - entrées + économies nettes
  // = ce qui sort réellement du compte (dépenses nettes de remboursements + épargne nette)
  var totalNetKpi = sortiesKpi - entreesKpi + econNetKpi;

  // Budget mois courant — toujours depuis _budgetCache[_curPeriod] (indépendant de la période affichée)
  var budgetTotal = 0, budgetDepense = 0;
  var _curPeriodCache = _budgetCache[_curPeriod];
  var _bdCats = _curPeriodCache
    ? (_curPeriodCache.buckets || [])
        .filter(function(b) { return !/to assign|à assigner|a assigner/i.test(b.bucket_name||''); })
        .reduce(function(acc, b) {
          return acc.concat((b.categories||[]).filter(function(c) { return c.category_id !== 'CAT001'; }));
        }, [])
    : null;
  if (!_bdCats && window._lastBudgetData) {
    _bdCats = window._lastBudgetData
      .filter(function(b) { return !/to assign|à assigner|a assigner/i.test(b.bucket_name||''); })
      .reduce(function(acc, b) {
        return acc.concat((b.categories||[]).filter(function(c) { return c.category_id !== 'CAT001'; }));
      }, []);
  }
  var reportTotal = 0;
  if (_bdCats) {
    _bdCats.forEach(function(c) {
      budgetTotal   += Number(c.budgeted) || 0; // toujours positif (allocation GAS)
      budgetDepense += Number(c.spent)    || 0; // net GAS : expenses (+) − remboursements/retraits (−)
      reportTotal   += Number(c.carry)    || 0; // cumulatif périodes antérieures (peut être négatif)
    });
  } else if (typeof loadBudget === 'function' && allPeriods && allPeriods.length) {
    // Budget jamais chargé → construire le select si vide, puis charger silencieusement
    setTimeout(function() {
      try {
        var _sel = document.getElementById('periodSelect');
        if (_sel && !_sel.value && typeof buildPeriodSelect === 'function') {
          buildPeriodSelect(allPeriods);
        }
        loadBudget(true, true);
      } catch(e){}
    }, 200);
  }
  // Capacité réelle = assigné + report M-1
  // Écart = spent GAS (net, même convention que budgeted) vs capacité GAS
  var _budgetCapacity = budgetTotal + reportTotal;
  var ecartDepBudget = _budgetCapacity > 0 ? (budgetDepense - _budgetCapacity) : null;

  // Solde prévisionnel (pointé + non-pointé = toutes TX)
  var soldePrev = (allTransactions || []).reduce(function(s, t) {
    return s - (Number(t.amount) || 0);
  }, 0);
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  // Total épargné all time : utiliser _totalSavings (valeur GAS, toujours à jour)
  var totalEpargne = _totalSavings || 0;

  // Par mois (pour meilleur mois + mois de suivi)
  var byMonth = {};
  txAll.forEach(function(t) {
    var p = _normalizePeriodKey(t.period); if (!p) return;
    if (!byMonth[p]) byMonth[p] = { rev:0, dep:0, sav:0 };
    var amt = Number(t.amount)||0;
    if (amt < 0) byMonth[p].rev += Math.abs(amt);
    else          byMonth[p].dep += amt;
    if (savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id))) byMonth[p].sav += amt; // somme directe (net)
  });
  var monthKeys   = Object.keys(byMonth).sort(function(a,b) { return _dbParseKey(a)-_dbParseKey(b); });
  var moisDeSuivi = monthKeys.length;

  // Meilleur mois épargne (par montant absolu)
  var bestMonth = null, bestSavAmt = 0;
  monthKeys.forEach(function(p) {
    if (byMonth[p].sav > bestSavAmt) { bestSavAmt = byMonth[p].sav; bestMonth = p; }
  });

  return {
    revenus, depenses, savingsFiltered,
    sortiesKpi, entreesKpi, econNetKpi, totalNetKpi,
    budgetTotal, budgetDepense, reportTotal, ecartDepBudget, soldePrev,
    totalEpargne, bestMonth, bestSavAmt, moisDeSuivi,
    txFiltered, txAll, byMonth, monthKeys, savingsIds, periodSet
  };
}

function _dbRenderKpis(data) {
  var fmt = function(v) { return formatEuro(Math.abs(v)); };

  // KPI 3 : Écart dépenses vs budget
  var ecartColor = 'var(--text-muted)', ecartVal = '—', ecartSub = 'Budget non disponible pour cette période';
  if (data.ecartDepBudget !== null) {
    var e = data.ecartDepBudget;
    ecartColor = e > 0 ? 'var(--red-alert)' : e < 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--text-muted)';
    ecartVal   = (e >= 0 ? '+' : '−') + fmt(e);
    ecartSub   = e > 0 ? 'Dépassement de budget ⚠️' : e < 0 ? 'Sous le budget 👍' : 'Budget respecté ✓';
  }

  var _econNet  = data.econNetKpi;
  var _econCol  = _econNet > 0 ? '#a78bfa' : _econNet < 0 ? 'var(--red-alert)' : 'var(--text-muted)';
  var _econPfx  = _econNet > 0 ? '+' : _econNet < 0 ? '−' : '';
  var _tNet     = data.totalNetKpi; // sorties − entrées + écon nettes
  var _tNetCol  = _tNet > 0 ? 'var(--red-alert)' : _tNet < 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--text-muted)';
  var _tNetPfx  = _tNet > 0 ? '−' : _tNet < 0 ? '+' : '';

  // 7 KPIs → PC : row1=4, row2=3, row3 masquée
  //           Mobile : row1=2, row2=2, row3=3
  var kpi1 = [
    { id:'kpi-sorties', icon:'💸', label:'Total sorties',
      value: data.sortiesKpi > 0 ? '−'+fmt(data.sortiesKpi) : '—',
      valueColor:'var(--red-alert)', sub: 'Dépenses hors épargne · mois en cours' },
    { id:'kpi-rev', icon:'💰', label:'Total entrées',
      value: data.entreesKpi > 0 ? '+'+fmt(data.entreesKpi) : '—',
      valueColor:'var(--green)', sub: 'Revenus + remboursements hors épargne · mois en cours' },
    { id:'kpi-econ', icon:'🐖', label:'Économies nettes',
      value: _econNet !== 0 ? _econPfx+fmt(Math.abs(_econNet)) : '—',
      valueColor: _econCol,
      sub: _econNet > 0 ? 'Mis de côté ce mois 👍' : _econNet < 0 ? 'Retrait épargne ce mois' : 'Mois en cours' },
    { id:'kpi-net', icon:'🔄', label:'Total net',
      value: _tNet !== 0 ? _tNetPfx+fmt(Math.abs(_tNet)) : '—',
      valueColor: _tNetCol, sub: 'Sorties − Entrées + Épargne · mois en cours' },
  ];

  var kpi2 = [
    { id:'kpi-bud', icon:'📋', label:'Total budget',
      value: (data.budgetTotal + data.reportTotal) > 0
        ? fmt(data.budgetTotal + data.reportTotal) + (data.reportTotal !== 0 ? '<sup style="font-size:9px;opacity:.65;vertical-align:super;">*</sup>' : '')
        : '—',
      valueColor:'var(--text)',
      sub: (data.budgetTotal + data.reportTotal) > 0
        ? (data.reportTotal !== 0
            ? '* dont '+(data.reportTotal > 0 ? '+' : '−')+fmt(data.reportTotal)+' report M-1'
            : fmt(data.budgetDepense)+' dépensé · mois en cours')
        : 'Mois en cours' },
    { id:'kpi-ecart', icon:'📊', label:'Écart budget / réel',
      value: ecartVal, valueColor: ecartColor,
      sub: ecartSub ? ecartSub+' · mois en cours' : 'Mois en cours' },
    { id:'kpi-solde', icon:'🏦', label:'Solde prévisionnel',
      value: (data.soldePrev >= 0 ? '+' : '−') + fmt(data.soldePrev),
      valueColor: data.soldePrev >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red-alert)',
      sub: data.totalEpargne > 0 ? '🐖 '+fmt(data.totalEpargne)+' épargnés all-time' : 'Pointé + non-pointé · toutes TX' },
  ];

  // Desktop : row1=4, row2=3, row3 masquée
  // Mobile  : row1=2 [Sorties|Entrées], row2=2 [Économies|Net], row3=3 [Budget|Écart|Solde]
  var kpi3mobile = kpi2; // les 3 KPIs budget passent en row3 sur mobile
  var kpi2mobile = [kpi1[2], kpi1[3]]; // Économies + Net
  var kpi1mobile = [kpi1[0], kpi1[1]]; // Sorties + Entrées

  function makeCard(k) {
    return '<div class="db-kpi-card" id="'+k.id+'" onclick="_dbShowDetail(\''+k.id+'\')">' +
      '<div class="db-kpi-label">'+k.icon+' '+k.label+'</div>' +
      '<div class="db-kpi-value" style="color:'+k.valueColor+';">'+k.value+'</div>' +
      '<div class="db-kpi-sub">'+k.sub+'</div>' +
    '</div>';
  }

  var r1 = document.getElementById('dbKpiRow1');
  var r2 = document.getElementById('dbKpiRow2');
  var r3 = document.getElementById('dbKpiRow3');
  var isDesktop = window.innerWidth >= 1024;
  if (r1) {
    r1.style.gridTemplateColumns = isDesktop ? 'repeat(4,1fr)' : 'repeat(2,1fr)';
    r1.innerHTML = (isDesktop ? kpi1 : kpi1mobile).map(makeCard).join('');
  }
  if (r2) {
    r2.style.gridTemplateColumns = isDesktop ? 'repeat(3,1fr)' : 'repeat(2,1fr)';
    r2.innerHTML = (isDesktop ? kpi2 : kpi2mobile).map(makeCard).join('');
  }
  if (r3) {
    r3.style.gridTemplateColumns = isDesktop ? 'repeat(3,1fr)' : 'repeat(3,1fr)';
    r3.innerHTML = (isDesktop ? [] : kpi3mobile).map(function(k) {
      // Row3 mobile : valeur un peu plus petite (3 cols serrées)
      return '<div class="db-kpi-card db-kpi-card-sm" id="'+k.id+'" onclick="_dbShowDetail(\''+k.id+'\')">' +
        '<div class="db-kpi-label">'+k.icon+' '+k.label+'</div>' +
        '<div class="db-kpi-value" style="color:'+k.valueColor+';">'+k.value+'</div>' +
        '<div class="db-kpi-sub">'+k.sub+'</div>' +
      '</div>';
    }).join('');
    // Sur desktop row3 masquée, sur mobile affiche les 3 KPIs budget
    r3.style.display = isDesktop ? 'none' : 'grid';
  }
}

function _dbCloseDetail() {
  var panel = document.getElementById('dbDetailPanel');
  if (panel) panel.style.display = 'none';
  document.querySelectorAll('.db-kpi-card').forEach(function(c) { c.classList.remove('active'); });
  _dbDetailTarget = null;
}

function _dbShowDetail(kpiId) {
  if (_dbDetailTarget === kpiId) { _dbCloseDetail(); return; }
  _dbDetailTarget = kpiId;
  document.querySelectorAll('.db-kpi-card').forEach(function(c) { c.classList.remove('active'); });
  var card = document.getElementById(kpiId);
  if (card) card.classList.add('active');
  // Fermer le panneau graphique quand un KPI est ouvert
  var chartDet = document.getElementById('dbChartDetail');
  if (chartDet) chartDet.style.display = 'none';

  var panel = document.getElementById('dbDetailPanel');
  if (!panel) return;
  var data = window._dbData;
  if (!data) return;
  var fmt = function(v) { return formatEuro(Math.abs(v)); };
  var html = '';

  if (kpiId === 'kpi-sorties') {
    var byBucket = {};
    // Dépenses brutes hors épargne (amount > 0) — même logique que le donut
    data.txFiltered.filter(function(t) {
      if (Number(t.amount) <= 0) return false;
      return !(data.savingsIds && data.savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id)));
    }).forEach(function(t) {
      var bkt = _dbGetBucketForCat(t.category_id) || 'Autre';
      if (/to assign|à assigner|a assigner|non assign/i.test(bkt) || String(t.category_id) === 'CAT001') return;
      byBucket[bkt] = (byBucket[bkt] || 0) + Number(t.amount);
    });
    var sortedBkt = Object.entries(byBucket).sort(function(a,b) { return b[1]-a[1]; });
    var totalDep = sortedBkt.reduce(function(s, e) { return s + e[1]; }, 0);
    html = '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px;font-size:13px;">💸 Sorties par groupe · mois en cours</div>';
    if (!sortedBkt.length) {
      html += '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Aucune dépense sur la période.</p>';
    } else {
      sortedBkt.forEach(function(entry) {
        var bkt = entry[0], amt = entry[1];
        var pct = totalDep > 0 ? Math.round(amt/totalDep*100) : 0;
        html += '<div style="margin-bottom:9px;">' +
          '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:3px;">' +
            '<span>'+bkt+'</span>' +
            '<span style="font-weight:700;">−'+fmt(amt)+'<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-weight:400;"> ('+pct+'%)</span></span>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<div style="height:4px;background:var(--segment);border-radius:2px;">' +
            '<div style="height:4px;background:var(--primary);border-radius:2px;width:'+pct+'%;"></div>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';
      });
    }
  }
  else if (kpiId === 'kpi-rev') {
    // Détail des entrées du mois courant, hors épargne (revenus + remboursements)
    var _curPer = _dbCurrentPeriod();
    var txRev = (data.txAll || []).filter(function(t) {
      if (_normalizePeriodKey(t.period) !== _curPer) return false;
      if (Number(t.amount) >= 0) return false;
      return !(data.savingsIds && data.savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id)));
    });
    txRev.sort(function(a,b) { return Number(a.amount) - Number(b.amount); }); // plus grosses entrées d'abord
    html = '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px;font-size:13px;">💰 Entrées du mois en cours</div>';
    if (!txRev.length) {
      html += '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Aucune entrée ce mois.</p>';
    } else {
      txRev.forEach(function(t) {
        var amt = Math.abs(Number(t.amount));
        var desc   = (t.description || '').trim();
        var debtor = (t.debtor || '').trim();
        var label  = desc || debtor || '—';
        var cat    = _dbGetCatName(t);
        var bkt    = _dbGetBucketForCat(t.category_id) || '';
        var isRev  = /to assign|à assigner|a assigner|non assign/i.test(bkt) || String(t.category_id) === 'CAT001';
        var tag    = isRev ? '💵 Revenu' : '↩ Remboursement';
        var tagCol = isRev ? 'var(--green)' : '#60A5FA';
        var _dr = (t.date || '').trim();
        var _dp = _dr.split('/');
        var dateStr = _dp.length === 3 ? _dp[0]+'/'+_dp[1]
                    : _dr.length >= 10 ? _dr.slice(8,10)+'/'+_dr.slice(5,7) : _dr;
        html += '<div class="db-detail-row">' +
          '<span style="color:var(--green);font-weight:700;min-width:75px;flex-shrink:0;">+'+fmt(amt)+'</span>' +
          '<span style="flex:1;overflow:hidden;min-width:0;">' +
            '<div style="overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;font-size:12px;">'+label+'</div>' +
            '<div style="font-size:10px;color:'+tagCol+';">'+tag+(cat ? ' · '+cat : '')+'</div>' +
          '</span>' +
          '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:10px;min-width:40px;text-align:right;flex-shrink:0;">'+dateStr+'</span>' +
        '</div>';
      });
    }
  }
  else if (kpiId === 'kpi-econ') {
    // Détail des transactions épargne du mois courant
    var _curPer2 = _dbCurrentPeriod();
    var txEcon = (data.txAll || []).filter(function(t) {
      return _normalizePeriodKey(t.period) === _curPer2
          && (data.savingsIds && data.savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id)));
    }).sort(function(a,b) { return Number(b.amount) - Number(a.amount); });
    var _totalEconMois = txEcon.reduce(function(s,t){ return s + (Number(t.amount)||0); }, 0);
    html = '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px;font-size:13px;">🐖 Transactions épargne · mois en cours</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-muted);margin-bottom:10px;">Net : ' +
        '<span style="color:'+(_totalEconMois>0?'#a78bfa':_totalEconMois<0?'var(--red-alert)':'var(--text-muted)')+';font-weight:700;">' +
        (_totalEconMois>0?'+':_totalEconMois<0?'−':'')+fmt(Math.abs(_totalEconMois))+'</span></div>';
    if (!txEcon.length) {
      html += '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Aucune transaction épargne ce mois.</p>';
    } else {
      txEcon.forEach(function(t) {
        var amt = Number(t.amount);
        var isDepot = amt > 0;
        var color = isDepot ? '#a78bfa' : 'var(--red-alert)';
        var prefix = isDepot ? '+' : '−';
        var tag = isDepot ? '💜 Dépôt' : '↩ Retrait';
        var desc = (t.description||'').trim() || (t.debtor||'').trim() || '—';
        var _dr = (t.date||'').trim(), _dp = _dr.split('/');
        var dateStr = _dp.length===3 ? _dp[0]+'/'+_dp[1] : _dr.length>=10 ? _dr.slice(8,10)+'/'+_dr.slice(5,7) : _dr;
        html += '<div class="db-detail-row">' +
          '<span style="color:'+color+';font-weight:700;min-width:75px;flex-shrink:0;">'+prefix+fmt(Math.abs(amt))+'</span>' +
          '<span style="flex:1;overflow:hidden;min-width:0;">' +
            '<div style="overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;font-size:12px;">'+desc+'</div>' +
            '<div style="font-size:10px;color:'+color+';">'+tag+'</div>' +
          '</span>' +
          '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:10px;min-width:40px;text-align:right;flex-shrink:0;">'+dateStr+'</span>' +
        '</div>';
      });
    }
  }
  else if (kpiId === 'kpi-bud') {
    if (!window._lastBudgetData) {
      html = '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Données budget non disponibles — ouvrir l\'onglet Budget d\'abord.</p>';
    } else {
      html = '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px;font-size:13px;">📋 Utilisation du budget par catégorie</div>';
      window._lastBudgetData.forEach(function(b) {
        if (/to assign|à assigner|a assigner/i.test(b.bucket_name||'')) return;
        (b.categories||[]).filter(function(c) { return Math.abs(Number(c.budgeted)||0) > 0; }).forEach(function(c) {
          var spent = Math.abs(Number(c.spent)||0);
          var bud   = Math.abs(Number(c.budgeted)||0);
          var pct   = Math.min(Math.round(spent/bud*100), 150);
          var color = pct > 100 ? 'var(--red-alert)' : pct > 80 ? '#F59E0B' : 'var(--green)';
          html += '<div style="margin-bottom:7px;">' +
            '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:11px;margin-bottom:2px;">' +
              '<span>'+c.category_name+'</span>' +
              '<span style="color:'+color+';font-weight:700;">'+pct+'%<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-weight:400;"> ('+fmt(spent)+'/'+fmt(bud)+')</span></span>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div style="height:3px;background:var(--segment);border-radius:2px;">' +
              '<div style="height:3px;background:'+color+';border-radius:2px;width:'+Math.min(pct,100)+'%;"></div>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>';
        });
      });
    }
  }
  else if (kpiId === 'kpi-ecart') {
    if (data.ecartDepBudget === null) {
      html = '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Données budget disponibles pour le mois courant ou précédent uniquement.</p>';
    } else {
      var over = [], under = [];
      (window._lastBudgetData||[]).forEach(function(b) {
        if (/to assign|à assigner|a assigner/i.test(b.bucket_name||'')) return;
        (b.categories||[]).forEach(function(c) {
          var spent = Math.abs(Number(c.spent)||0);
          var bud   = Math.abs(Number(c.budgeted)||0);
          if (!bud) return;
          var ecart = spent - bud;
          if (ecart > 0.5) over.push({ name:c.category_name, ecart:ecart });
          else if (ecart < -5) under.push({ name:c.category_name, ecart:ecart });
        });
      });
      html = '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:10px;font-size:13px;">📊 Écarts par catégorie</div>';
      if (over.length) {
        html += '<div style="color:var(--red-alert);font-size:11px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;">⚠️ Dépassements ('+over.length+')</div>';
        over.sort(function(a,b){ return b.ecart-a.ecart; }).forEach(function(c) {
          html += '<div class="db-detail-row"><span style="flex:1;font-size:12px;">'+c.name+'</span><span style="color:var(--red-alert);font-weight:700;min-width:70px;text-align:right;">+'+fmt(c.ecart)+'</span></div>';
        });
      }
      if (under.length) {
        html += '<div style="color:var(--green);font-size:11px;font-weight:700;margin:'+(over.length?'10px':'0')+' 0 6px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;">✅ Sous budget ('+under.length+')</div>';
        under.sort(function(a,b){ return a.ecart-b.ecart; }).forEach(function(c) {
          html += '<div class="db-detail-row"><span style="flex:1;font-size:12px;">'+c.name+'</span><span style="color:var(--green);font-weight:700;min-width:70px;text-align:right;">'+fmt(c.ecart)+'</span></div>';
        });
      }
      if (!over.length && !under.length) html += '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Toutes les catégories sont dans le budget 🎉</p>';
    }
  }
  else if (kpiId === 'kpi-totalEp') {
    var savByMonth = {};
    (allTransactions||[]).forEach(function(t) {
      if (!data.savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id))) return;
      var p = _normalizePeriodKey(t.period); if (!p) return;
      savByMonth[p] = (savByMonth[p]||0) + Math.abs(Number(t.amount)||0);
    });
    var sortedMonths = Object.entries(savByMonth).sort(function(a,b){ return _dbParseKey(b[0])-_dbParseKey(a[0]); });
    html = '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:10px;font-size:13px;">🐖 Épargne mensuelle — toutes périodes</div>';
    var runCumul = sortedMonths.reduce(function(s,e){ return s+e[1]; }, 0);
    sortedMonths.forEach(function(entry) {
      var p = entry[0], amt = entry[1];
      html += '<div class="db-detail-row">' +
        '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);min-width:62px;font-size:11px;">'+p+'</span>' +
        '<span style="color:#a78bfa;font-weight:700;min-width:80px;">+'+fmt(amt)+'</span>' +
        '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:10px;">cumul : '+fmt(runCumul)+'</span>' +
      '</div>';
      runCumul -= amt;
    });
    if (!sortedMonths.length) html += '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Aucun versement épargne.</p>';
  }
  else if (kpiId === 'kpi-best') {
    var rates = data.monthKeys.map(function(p) {
      var d = data.byMonth[p];
      return { p:p, sav:d.sav, rev:d.rev };
    }).filter(function(r){ return r.sav > 0; }).sort(function(a,b){ return b.sav-a.sav; }).slice(0,5);
    var medals = ['🥇','🥈','🥉','4️⃣','5️⃣'];
    html = '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:10px;font-size:13px;">🏆 Top 5 mois (par montant épargné)</div>';
    rates.forEach(function(r, i) {
      var taux = r.rev > 0 ? Math.round(r.sav/r.rev*100) : 0;
      html += '<div class="db-detail-row">' +
        '<span style="font-size:18px;flex-shrink:0;">'+medals[i]+'</span>' +
        '<span style="min-width:62px;font-size:12px;color:var(--text-muted);">'+r.p+'</span>' +
        '<span style="font-weight:800;color:#a78bfa;min-width:80px;">+'+fmt(r.sav)+'</span>' +
        '<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-muted);">'+taux+'% du revenu</span>' +
      '</div>';
    });
    if (!rates.length) html += '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Aucune donnée.</p>';
  }
  else if (kpiId === 'kpi-solde') {
    var _sp = 0, _snp = 0;
    (allTransactions||[]).forEach(function(t) {
      var val = -(Number(t.amount)||0);
      if (t.cleared || t.locked) _sp  += val;
      else                        _snp += val;
    });
    var _sc = function(v) { return v >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red-alert)'; };
    var _sf = function(v) { return (v >= 0 ? '+' : '−') + fmt(v); };
    html = '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px;font-size:13px;">🏦 Détail du solde prévisionnel</div>' +
      '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:10px;">' +
        '<div style="background:var(--surface);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;">' +
          '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;">✓ Pointé</div>' +
          '<div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;margin-top:4px;color:'+_sc(_sp)+';">'+_sf(_sp)+'</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div style="background:var(--surface);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;">' +
          '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;">○ Non pointé</div>' +
          '<div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;margin-top:4px;color:'+_sc(_snp)+';">'+_sf(_snp)+'</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div style="background:var(--surface);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;">' +
          '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;">⟳ Prévisionnel</div>' +
          '<div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;margin-top:4px;color:'+_sc(data.soldePrev)+';">'+_sf(data.soldePrev)+'</div>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</div>';
  }

  panel.innerHTML = html;
  panel.style.display = 'block';
  panel.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'nearest' });
}

// ── Cross-filter (Power BI style) ─────────────────────────────────
function _dbResetFilter() {
  _dbCrossFilter = { type: null, value: null };
  var badge = document.getElementById('dbFilterBadge');
  if (badge) badge.style.display = 'none';
  var detail = document.getElementById('dbChartDetail');
  if (detail) detail.style.display = 'none';
}

function _dbSetFilter(type, value) {
  if (_dbCrossFilter.type === type && _dbCrossFilter.value === value) {
    _dbClearFilter(); return;
  }
  _dbCrossFilter = { type: type, value: value };
  var badge = document.getElementById('dbFilterBadge');
  var badgeText = document.getElementById('dbFilterBadgeText');
  if (badge && badgeText) {
    badgeText.textContent = (type === 'bucket' ? '🗂 ' : '📌 ') + value;
    badge.style.display = 'inline-flex';
  }
  _dbCloseDetail();
  var data = window._dbData;
  if (!data) return;
  _dbRenderDonut(data);
  _dbRenderCatChart(data);
  _dbShowChartDetail(type, value);
}

function _dbClearFilter() {
  _dbCrossFilter = { type: null, value: null };
  var badge = document.getElementById('dbFilterBadge');
  if (badge) badge.style.display = 'none';
  var detail = document.getElementById('dbChartDetail');
  if (detail) detail.style.display = 'none';
  var data = window._dbData;
  if (!data) return;
  _dbRenderDonut(data);
  _dbRenderCatChart(data);
}

function _dbGetCatName(t) {
  // t.category est résolu par _normalizeTx à partir d'allCategories (plan courant) — source de vérité
  if (t.category && !/^CAT[-\d]/.test(t.category)) return t.category;
  // Fallback : chercher directement dans allCategories (let global, pas window.)
  var catId = String(t.category_id || '');
  if (catId && allCategories && allCategories.length) {
    var cat = allCategories.find(function(c) {
      return String(c.id) === catId;
    });
    if (cat && cat.name) return cat.name;
  }
  return t.category || catId || '—';
}

function _dbShowChartDetail(type, value) {
  var detail = document.getElementById('dbChartDetail');
  if (!detail || !window._dbData) return;
  var data = window._dbData;
  var fmt = function(v) { return formatEuro(Math.abs(v)); };

  // Net hors épargne (dépenses + remboursements, hors revenus) — cohérent avec donut et barres
  var txF = data.txFiltered.filter(function(t) {
    var amt = Number(t.amount) || 0;
    if (amt === 0) return false;
    if (data.savingsIds && data.savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id))) return false;
    var bkt = _dbGetBucketForCat(t.category_id) || 'Autre';
    if (/to assign|à assigner|a assigner|non assign/i.test(bkt) || String(t.category_id) === 'CAT001') return false;
    if (type === 'bucket')   return (_dbGetBucketForCat(t.category_id)||'Autre') === value;
    if (type === 'category') return _dbGetCatName(t) === value;
    return true;
  });
  // Dépenses d'abord, remboursements ensuite
  txF.sort(function(a,b) { return Number(b.amount) - Number(a.amount); });

  var total = txF.reduce(function(s,t) { return s + Number(t.amount); }, 0);
  var totalColor = total >= 0 ? 'var(--red-alert)' : '#60A5FA';
  var totalPrefix = total >= 0 ? '−' : '+';
  var html = '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px;">' +
    '<div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;">'+(type==='bucket'?'🗂 ':'📌 ')+value+'</div>' +
    '<div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:800;color:'+totalColor+';">'+totalPrefix+fmt(Math.abs(total))+'</div>' +
  '</div>';

  if (!txF.length) {
    html += '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Aucune dépense pour ce filtre sur la période sélectionnée.</p>';
  } else {
    txF.slice(0,30).forEach(function(t) {
      var catName = _dbGetCatName(t);
      var desc   = (t.description || '').trim();
      var debtor = (t.debtor || '').trim();
      var mainLabel = desc || debtor || '—';
      var subLabel  = desc && debtor ? debtor : '';
      // Formatage date → DD/MM
      var _dr = (t.date || '').trim();
      var _dp = _dr.split('/');
      var dateStr = _dp.length === 3 ? _dp[0]+'/'+_dp[1]
                  : _dr.length >= 10 ? _dr.slice(8,10)+'/'+_dr.slice(5,7)
                  : _dr;
      var _ta = Number(t.amount), _isRemb = _ta < 0;
      html += '<div class="db-detail-row">' +
        '<span style="color:'+(_isRemb?'var(--green)':'var(--red-alert)')+';font-weight:700;min-width:75px;flex-shrink:0;">'+(_isRemb?'+':'−')+fmt(Math.abs(_ta))+'</span>' +
        '<span style="flex:1;overflow:hidden;min-width:0;">' +
          '<div style="overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;font-size:12px;">'+mainLabel+'</div>' +
          (subLabel ? '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-muted);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">👤 '+subLabel+'</div>' : '') +
        '</span>' +
        '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:10px;min-width:72px;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0;">' +
          (dateStr ? '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text);">'+dateStr+'</div>' : '') +
          '<div style="font-size:9px;opacity:0.7;">'+catName+'</div>' +
        '</span>' +
      '</div>';
    });
    if (txF.length > 30) {
      html += '<div style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;padding-top:8px;font-size:11px;">+' + (txF.length-30) + ' autres transactions</div>';
    }
  }

  detail.innerHTML = html;
  detail.style.display = 'block';
  detail.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'nearest' });
}

function _dbGetBucketForCat(catId) {
  // Priorité : allCategories + allBuckets (let globaux, pas window.)
  if (allCategories && allCategories.length && allBuckets && allBuckets.length) {
    var cat = allCategories.find(function(c) {
      return String(c.id||c.category_id) === String(catId);
    });
    if (cat && cat.bucket_id) {
      var bkt = allBuckets.find(function(b) { return String(b.id) === String(cat.bucket_id); });
      if (bkt) return bkt.name || bkt.bucket_name || 'Autre';
    }
  }
  // Fallback : données budget (si Budget tab déjà ouvert)
  if (!window._lastBudgetData) return 'Autre';
  for (var i = 0; i < window._lastBudgetData.length; i++) {
    var b = window._lastBudgetData[i];
    var cats = b.categories || [];
    for (var j = 0; j < cats.length; j++) {
      if (String(cats[j].category_id) === String(catId)) return b.bucket_name || b.name || 'Autre';
    }
  }
  return 'Autre';
}

function _dbRenderDonut(data) {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('dbDonutCanvas');
  var legEl  = document.getElementById('dbDonutLegend');
  if (!canvas || !legEl) return;

  // Net par bucket hors épargne (dépenses − remboursements) → donut
  // Segments négatifs exclus (remboursements > dépenses dans ce bucket)
  var byBucket = {};
  data.txFiltered.forEach(function(t) {
    var amt = Number(t.amount) || 0;
    if (amt === 0) return;
    if (data.savingsIds && data.savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id))) return;
    var bkt = _dbGetBucketForCat(t.category_id) || 'Autre';
    if (/to assign|à assigner|a assigner|non assign/i.test(bkt) || String(t.category_id) === 'CAT001') return;
    byBucket[bkt] = (byBucket[bkt] || 0) + amt;
  });

  var items = Object.entries(byBucket).filter(function(e){ return e[1] > 0; }).sort(function(a,b){ return b[1]-a[1]; });
  var total = items.reduce(function(s,e){ return s+e[1]; }, 0);

  var _prim = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--primary').trim() || '#6366F1';
  var palette = [_prim,'#10B981','#F59E0B','#EF4444','#3B82F6','#EC4899','#8B5CF6','#14B8A6','#F97316','#84CC16'];
  // devicePixelRatio pour canvas net (pas flou sur écrans HiDPI/Retina)
  var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  var isMobile = window.innerWidth < 1024;
  // Sur mobile : donut pleine largeur de la carte (scrollable si grand), sinon 220px fixe
  var cssSize = isMobile ? Math.min((canvas.parentElement ? canvas.parentElement.clientWidth : W) - 20, 300) : 220;
  canvas.style.width  = cssSize + 'px';
  canvas.style.height = cssSize + 'px';
  canvas.width  = cssSize * dpr;
  canvas.height = cssSize * dpr;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cssSize, cssSize);
  var size = cssSize;

  if (!items.length) {
    legEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px;">Aucune dépense sur la période.</span>';
    canvas.onclick = null; return;
  }

  var activeFilter = _dbCrossFilter.type === 'bucket' ? _dbCrossFilter.value : null;
  var cx = size/2, cy = size/2, r = size/2-6, inner = r*0.52;
  var angle = -Math.PI/2;
  var slices = [];

  items.forEach(function(item, i) {
    var sweep = total > 0 ? (item[1]/total)*2*Math.PI : 0;
    var isActive = !activeFilter || item[0] === activeFilter;
    ctx.globalAlpha = isActive ? 1.0 : 0.22;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx,cy);
    ctx.arc(cx,cy,r,angle,angle+sweep); ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle = palette[i%palette.length]; ctx.fill();
    if (isActive && activeFilter) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
    }
    slices.push({ name:item[0], value:item[1], color:palette[i%palette.length], startAngle:angle, sweep:sweep, pct:item[1]/total });
    angle += sweep;
  });
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  var bgColor = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--sheet').trim() || '#1a1a2e';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx,cy,inner,0,2*Math.PI); ctx.fillStyle = bgColor; ctx.fill();

  var centerVal = activeFilter ? (byBucket[activeFilter]||0) : total;
  var textColor = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--text').trim() || '#fff';
  var fCenter = isMobile ? 'bold 26px' : 'bold 11px';
  ctx.fillStyle = textColor; ctx.font = fCenter + ' system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText(formatEuro(centerVal).replace(/[\s\u00a0]/g,''), cx, cy);

  // Légende en grille 2 colonnes sous le donut
  var legItemSize = isMobile ? '18px' : '10px';
  var legDotSize  = isMobile ? '15px' : '8px';
  var legPad      = isMobile ? '9px 12px' : '3px 5px';
  legEl.style.display = 'grid';
  legEl.style.gridTemplateColumns = 'repeat(2,1fr)';
  legEl.style.gap = isMobile ? '9px' : '3px';
  legEl.innerHTML = items.slice(0,8).map(function(item, i) {
    var pct = Math.round(item[1]/total*100);
    var isSelected = activeFilter === item[0];
    var esc = item[0].replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'");
    return '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;cursor:pointer;padding:'+legPad+';border-radius:6px;'+(isSelected?'background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);':'')+';opacity:'+(!activeFilter||isSelected?1:0.35)+';transition:opacity .2s;" onclick="_dbSetFilter(\'bucket\',\''+esc+'\')">' +
      '<span style="width:'+legDotSize+';height:'+legDotSize+';border-radius:2px;background:'+palette[i%palette.length]+';flex-shrink:0;display:inline-block;"></span>' +
      '<span style="flex:1;font-size:'+legItemSize+';overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">'+item[0]+'</span>' +
      '<span style="font-size:'+legItemSize+';font-weight:700;color:var(--text-muted);flex-shrink:0;">'+pct+'%</span>' +
    '</div>';
  }).join('');

  window._dbDonutSlices = slices;

  canvas.onmousemove = function(e) {
    var tip = document.getElementById('dbTooltip'); if (!tip) return;
    var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var x = (e.clientX-rect.left)*(cssSize/rect.width)-cx, y = (e.clientY-rect.top)*(cssSize/rect.height)-cy;
    var dist = Math.sqrt(x*x+y*y);
    if (dist < inner || dist > r) { tip.style.display='none'; return; }
    var a = Math.atan2(y,x); if (a < -Math.PI/2) a += 2*Math.PI;
    var hit = null;
    (window._dbDonutSlices||[]).forEach(function(s){ if (a >= s.startAngle && a < s.startAngle+s.sweep) hit=s; });
    if (hit) {
      tip.innerHTML = '<span style="display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:2px;background:'+hit.color+';margin-right:5px;vertical-align:middle;"></span><strong>'+hit.name+'</strong><br><span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px;">'+formatEuro(hit.value)+' · '+Math.round(hit.pct*100)+'%</span>';
      tip.style.display='block'; tip.style.left=(e.clientX+14)+'px'; tip.style.top=(e.clientY-50)+'px';
    } else { tip.style.display='none'; }
  };
  canvas.onmouseleave = function(){ var tip=document.getElementById('dbTooltip'); if(tip) tip.style.display='none'; };
  canvas.onclick = function(e) {
    var rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var x=(e.clientX-rect.left)*(cssSize/rect.width)-cx, y=(e.clientY-rect.top)*(cssSize/rect.height)-cy;
    var dist=Math.sqrt(x*x+y*y);
    if (dist < inner || dist > r) { _dbClearFilter(); return; }
    var a=Math.atan2(y,x); if (a < -Math.PI/2) a += 2*Math.PI;
    var hit=null;
    (window._dbDonutSlices||[]).forEach(function(s){ if (a >= s.startAngle && a < s.startAngle+s.sweep) hit=s; });
    if (hit) _dbSetFilter('bucket', hit.name); else _dbClearFilter();
  };
}

function _dbRenderSavingsCurve(data) {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('dbSavingsCurve');
  if (!canvas) return;
  var W = canvas.clientWidth;
  if (!W || W < 10) { setTimeout(function(){ _dbRenderSavingsCurve(data||window._dbData); }, 80); return; }

  var isMobileSv = window.innerWidth < 1024;
  var H = isMobileSv ? 260 : 200;
  var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  canvas.style.width  = W + 'px';
  canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
  canvas.width  = W * dpr;
  canvas.height = H * dpr;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // Polices/épaisseurs mobiles (roadmap #24 : textes canvas illisibles à bout de bras)
  var fSvEmpty = isMobileSv ? '17px'      : '12px';
  var fSvLast  = isMobileSv ? 'bold 19px' : 'bold 10px';
  var fSvAxis  = isMobileSv ? '14px'      : '9px';
  var svDotR   = isMobileSv ? 4.5 : 3;
  var svLineW  = isMobileSv ? 3   : 2;

  var textColor  = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--text').trim()       || '#fff';
  var textMuted  = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#888';
  var _prim      = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--primary').trim()    || '#6366F1';

  // Épargne cumulée mois par mois (somme directe, convention WIMM : dépôt = amount positif dans catégorie épargne)
  var savingsIds = data.savingsIds;
  var byMonth = {}; // period → net épargne ce mois
  (data.txAll || []).forEach(function(t) {
    if (!savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id))) return;
    var p = _normalizePeriodKey(t.period); if (!p) return;
    byMonth[p] = (byMonth[p] || 0) + (Number(t.amount) || 0);
  });

  var months = Object.keys(byMonth).sort(function(a,b) { return _dbParseKey(a)-_dbParseKey(b); });
  // Limiter aux 12 derniers mois
  if (months.length > 12) months = months.slice(months.length - 12);

  if (!months.length) {
    ctx.fillStyle = textMuted;
    ctx.font = fSvEmpty + ' system-ui';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText('Aucune transaction épargne', W/2, H/2);
    return;
  }

  // Construire la courbe cumulative
  var cumul = 0;
  var points = months.map(function(p) {
    cumul += byMonth[p];
    return { period: p, val: cumul };
  });

  var padL = isMobileSv ? 68 : 44, padR = 10, padT = isMobileSv ? 20 : 14, padB = isMobileSv ? 38 : 28;
  var chartW = W - padL - padR;
  var chartH = H - padT - padB;

  var vals = points.map(function(p){ return p.val; });
  var minVal = Math.min(0, Math.min.apply(null, vals));
  var maxVal = Math.max.apply(null, vals);
  if (maxVal === minVal) { maxVal = minVal + 1; }

  function xOf(i) { return padL + (i / Math.max(points.length - 1, 1)) * chartW; }
  function yOf(v) { return padT + chartH - ((v - minVal) / (maxVal - minVal)) * chartH; }

  // Ligne zéro
  var y0 = yOf(0);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(128,128,128,0.2)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(padL, y0); ctx.lineTo(W - padR, y0); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  // Aire de remplissage sous la courbe
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(xOf(0), y0);
  points.forEach(function(p, i) { ctx.lineTo(xOf(i), yOf(p.val)); });
  ctx.lineTo(xOf(points.length - 1), y0);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba('+_hexToRgb(_prim)+',0.15)';
  ctx.fill();

  // Courbe
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.strokeStyle = _prim;
  ctx.lineWidth = svLineW;
  ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  points.forEach(function(p, i) {
    i === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(xOf(i), yOf(p.val)) : ctx.lineTo(xOf(i), yOf(p.val));
  });
  ctx.stroke();

  // Points + valeurs finales
  points.forEach(function(p, i) {
    var x = xOf(i), y = yOf(p.val);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, svDotR, 0, 2*Math.PI);
    ctx.fillStyle = _prim;
    ctx.fill();
  });

  // Valeur finale bien visible
  var last = points[points.length - 1];
  ctx.fillStyle = _prim;
  ctx.font = fSvLast + ' system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'right';
  ctx.textBaseline = last.val >= 0 ? 'bottom' : 'top';
  ctx.fillText(formatEuro(last.val).replace(/[\s\u00a0]/g,''), W - padR, last.val >= 0 ? yOf(last.val) - 2 : yOf(last.val) + 2);

  // Labels mois (axe X) — n'afficher que si pas trop serré
  ctx.fillStyle = textMuted;
  ctx.font = fSvAxis + ' system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
  var step = points.length <= 6 ? 1 : points.length <= 12 ? (isMobileSv ? 3 : 2) : (isMobileSv ? 4 : 3);
  points.forEach(function(p, i) {
    if (i % step !== 0 && i !== points.length - 1) return;
    var parts = p.period.split('-');
    var label = parts.length === 2 ? parts[1]+'/'+parts[0].slice(2) : p.period;
    ctx.fillText(label, xOf(i), H - padB + 4);
  });

  // Axe Y — valeur min et max
  ctx.fillStyle = textMuted;
  ctx.font = fSvAxis + ' system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'right';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText(formatEuro(maxVal).replace(/[\s\u00a0]/g,''), padL - 3, padT);
  if (minVal < 0) ctx.fillText(formatEuro(minVal).replace(/[\s\u00a0]/g,''), padL - 3, H - padB);

  // Tooltip au survol
  canvas.onmousemove = function(e) {
    var tip = document.getElementById('dbTooltip');
    if (!tip) return;
    var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var mx = (e.clientX - rect.left) * (W / rect.width);
    // Trouver le point le plus proche
    var closest = null, minDist = 999;
    points.forEach(function(p, i) {
      var d = Math.abs(xOf(i) - mx);
      if (d < minDist) { minDist = d; closest = p; }
    });
    if (closest && minDist < chartW / points.length) {
      tip.innerHTML = '<strong>'+closest.period+'</strong><br>Cumulé : <span style="color:'+_prim+'">'+formatEuro(closest.val)+'</span><br>Ce mois : <span style="color:var(--text-muted);">'+formatEuro(byMonth[closest.period])+'</span>';
      tip.style.display = 'block';
      tip.style.left = (e.clientX + 14) + 'px';
      tip.style.top  = (e.clientY - 60) + 'px';
    } else { tip.style.display = 'none'; }
  };
  canvas.onmouseleave = function() {
    var tip = document.getElementById('dbTooltip');
    if (tip) tip.style.display = 'none';
  };
  canvas.onclick = null;
}

// ─── Budget vs Dépensé ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Retourne les catégories budget avec bucket_name inclus sur chaque entrée
function _dbGetBudgetCatsWithBucket() {
  var src = window._lastBudgetData;
  if (!src) return null;
  return src
    .filter(function(b) { return !/^(to assign|à assigner|a assigner|non assign)/i.test((b.bucket_name||'').trim()); })
    .reduce(function(acc, b) {
      return acc.concat(
        (b.categories||[])
          .filter(function(c) { return c.category_id !== 'CAT001'; })
          .map(function(c) { return Object.assign({}, c, { bucket_name: b.bucket_name || '' }); })
      );
    }, []);
}

function _dbRenderBudgetChart(data) {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('dbBudgetCanvas');
  var legEl  = document.getElementById('dbBudgetLegend');
  if (!canvas) return;
  var W = canvas.clientWidth;
  if (!W || W < 10) { setTimeout(function(){ _dbRenderBudgetChart(data||window._dbData); }, 80); return; }

  var textColor = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--text').trim()       || '#fff';
  var textMuted = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#888';
  var GREEN = 'rgba(16,185,129,0.85)';
  var RED   = 'rgba(239,68,68,0.85)';
  var GREY  = 'rgba(128,128,128,0.22)';

  // Récupérer les catégories budget avec bucket_name
  var _bdCats = _dbGetBudgetCatsWithBucket();
  if (!_bdCats) _bdCats = window._budgetCatDetails || null;  // fallback sans bucket_name

  function _showEmpty(msg, height) {
    var H0 = height || 80, dpr0 = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    canvas.style.width = W+'px'; canvas.style.height = H0+'px';
    canvas.width = W*dpr0; canvas.height = H0*dpr0;
    var c0 = canvas.getContext('2d'); c0.scale(dpr0, dpr0);
    c0.fillStyle = textMuted; c0.font = (window.innerWidth < 1024 ? '16px' : '12px') + ' system-ui';
    c0.textAlign = 'center'; c0.textBaseline = 'middle';
    c0.fillText(msg, W/2, H0/2);
    if (legEl) legEl.innerHTML = '';
    canvas.onclick = null; canvas.onmousemove = null;
  }

  if (!_bdCats || !_bdCats.length) {
    _showEmpty('Budget non chargé — ouvrir l\'onglet Budget d\'abord');
    return;
  }

  // Cross-filter
  var activeFilter   = _dbCrossFilter.type;
  var activeValue    = _dbCrossFilter.value;

  // Construire la liste de base
  var allCats = _bdCats
    .filter(function(c) { return (Number(c.budgeted)||0) > 0 || (Number(c.spent)||0) > 0; })
    .map(function(c) {
      var budgeted = Number(c.budgeted) || 0;
      var carry    = Number(c.carry)    || 0;
      var spent    = Number(c.spent)    || 0;
      var capacity = budgeted + carry;
      return {
        name:       c.category_name || c.name || '?',
        bucket:     c.bucket_name || '',
        budgeted:   budgeted,
        carry:      carry,
        capacity:   capacity,
        spent:      spent,
        over:       spent > capacity && capacity > 0
      };
    })
    .sort(function(a,b) { return b.spent - a.spent; })
    .slice(0, window.innerWidth < 1024 ? 8 : 18);

  if (!allCats.length) {
    _showEmpty('Aucune dépense budgétée ce mois', 60);
    return;
  }

  // Appliquer filtre bucket : masquer les autres (dim plutôt que retirer → on garde toutes les lignes)
  // Appliquer filtre catégorie : idem (dim)
  var isMobileBud = window.innerWidth < 1024;
  var rowH = isMobileBud ? 72 : 28, padT = 12, padB = 24;
  var fLabelBud = isMobileBud ? '20px' : '10px';
  var fAmtBud   = isMobileBud ? '17px' : '9px';
  var fAmtSub   = isMobileBud ? '13px' : '8px'; // 2e ligne (%) sur mobile
  var maxLabelBud = isMobileBud ? 11 : 13;
  var labelW = isMobileBud ? Math.min(W * 0.37, 135) : Math.min(W * 0.30, 100);
  var amtW   = isMobileBud ? 75 : 62;
  var barAreaW = W - labelW - amtW - 6;
  var bh = rowH * 0.22;

  // Si filtre bucket actif, limiter aux cats de ce bucket
  var cats = allCats;
  if (activeFilter === 'bucket' && activeValue) {
    cats = allCats.filter(function(c) { return c.bucket === activeValue; });
    if (!cats.length) cats = allCats; // fallback
  }

  var maxVal = Math.max.apply(null, cats.map(function(c) { return Math.max(c.capacity, c.spent, 1); }));
  var H = cats.length * rowH + padT + padB;
  var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  canvas.style.width  = W + 'px';
  canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
  canvas.width  = W * dpr;
  canvas.height = H * dpr;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  cats.forEach(function(cat, i) {
    // Dim si filtre catégorie actif et pas la bonne
    var isSel = activeFilter === 'category' && activeValue === cat.name;
    var alpha = activeFilter === 'category' ? (isSel ? 1.0 : 0.28) : 1.0;
    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;

    var y = padT + i * rowH;
    var midY = y + rowH / 2;

    // Label (safe emoji truncation via spread)
    ctx.fillStyle = (isSel || !activeFilter) ? textColor : textMuted;
    ctx.font = (isSel ? 'bold ' : '') + fLabelBud + ' system-ui';
    ctx.textAlign = 'right';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    var _chars = [...cat.name];
    var label = _chars.length > maxLabelBud ? _chars.slice(0, maxLabelBud-1).join('')+'…' : cat.name;
    ctx.fillText(label, labelW - 4, midY);

    // Barre budget (gris, haut)
    var bwBudget = cat.capacity > 0 ? (cat.capacity / maxVal) * barAreaW : 0;
    ctx.fillStyle = GREY;
    if (bwBudget > 0) ctx.fillRect(labelW, midY - bh, bwBudget, bh);

    // Barre dépensé (colorée, bas)
    var bwSpent = cat.spent > 0 ? (cat.spent / maxVal) * barAreaW : 0;
    ctx.fillStyle = cat.over ? RED : GREEN;
    if (bwSpent > 0) ctx.fillRect(labelW, midY, bwSpent, bh);

    // Ligne limite budget
    if (cat.capacity > 0) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * 0.5;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(labelW + bwBudget, midY - bh - 1);
      ctx.lineTo(labelW + bwBudget, midY + bh + 1);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
    }

    // Montants : mobile = 2 lignes (dépensé / %) · desktop = "dépensé / budget"
    var spentStr = (cat.spent > 0 ? formatEuro(cat.spent) : '0€').replace(/[\s\u00a0]/g,'');
    var capStr   = cat.capacity > 0 ? formatEuro(cat.capacity).replace(/[\s\u00a0]/g,'') : '—';
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.fillStyle = cat.over ? RED : textMuted;
    if (isMobileBud) {
      // Ligne 1 : montant dépensé (gras, plus grand)
      ctx.font = 'bold ' + fAmtBud + ' system-ui';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.fillText(spentStr, labelW + barAreaW + 5, midY - rowH * 0.16);
      // Ligne 2 : % du budget (plus petit, discret)
      if (cat.capacity > 0) {
        ctx.font = fAmtSub + ' system-ui';
        ctx.fillStyle = textMuted;
        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * 0.75;
        ctx.fillText(Math.round(cat.spent / cat.capacity * 100) + '%', labelW + barAreaW + 5, midY + rowH * 0.2);
        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
      }
    } else {
      ctx.font = fAmtBud + ' system-ui';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.fillText(spentStr + ' / ' + capStr, labelW + barAreaW + 5, midY);
    }
  });
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  // Légende
  if (legEl) {
    var legFs = isMobileBud ? '16px' : '10px';
    var legDs = isMobileBud ? '16px' : '12px';
    legEl.style.fontSize = legFs;
    legEl.style.gap = isMobileBud ? '10px' : '8px';
    legEl.innerHTML =
      '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;">' +
        '<span style="width:'+legDs+';height:7px;background:rgba(128,128,128,0.35);display:inline-block;border-radius:2px;"></span> Budget</span>' +
      '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;">' +
        '<span style="width:'+legDs+';height:7px;background:'+GREEN+';display:inline-block;border-radius:2px;"></span> Dépensé ≤ budget</span>' +
      '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;">' +
        '<span style="width:'+legDs+';height:7px;background:'+RED+';display:inline-block;border-radius:2px;"></span> Dépassement</span>';
  }

  // Tooltip au survol + click cross-filter par catégorie
  canvas.onmousemove = function(e) {
    var tip = document.getElementById('dbTooltip');
    if (!tip) return;
    var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var localY = (e.clientY - rect.top) * (H / rect.height);
    var idx = Math.floor((localY - padT) / rowH);
    if (idx >= 0 && idx < cats.length) {
      var c = cats[idx];
      var rem = c.capacity - c.spent;
      var remStr = rem >= 0
        ? '<span style="color:'+GREEN+';">'+formatEuro(rem)+' restant</span>'
        : '<span style="color:'+RED+';">'+formatEuro(Math.abs(rem))+' dépassé</span>';
      tip.innerHTML =
        '<strong>'+c.name+'</strong>' +
        (c.bucket ? '<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-muted);">'+c.bucket+'</span>' : '') + '<br>' +
        'Budget : <span style="color:var(--text);">'+formatEuro(c.capacity)+'</span>' +
        (c.carry ? ' <small style="opacity:.65;">(+'+formatEuro(c.carry)+' report)</small>' : '') + '<br>' +
        'Dépensé : <span style="color:'+(c.over?RED:GREEN)+';">'+formatEuro(c.spent)+'</span><br>' +
        remStr;
      tip.style.display = 'block';
      tip.style.left = (e.clientX + 14) + 'px';
      tip.style.top  = (e.clientY - 80) + 'px';
    } else { tip.style.display = 'none'; }
  };
  canvas.onmouseleave = function() {
    var tip = document.getElementById('dbTooltip');
    if (tip) tip.style.display = 'none';
  };
  // Click : cross-filter par catégorie (filtre le donut + cat chart)
  canvas.onclick = function(e) {
    var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var localY = (e.clientY - rect.top) * (H / rect.height);
    var idx = Math.floor((localY - padT) / rowH);
    if (idx >= 0 && idx < cats.length) {
      var hit = cats[idx].name;
      if (_dbCrossFilter.type === 'category' && _dbCrossFilter.value === hit) _dbClearFilter();
      else _dbSetFilter('category', hit);
    } else { _dbClearFilter(); }
  };
}

// Helper : hex → "r,g,b" pour rgba()
function _hexToRgb(hex) {
  var h = hex.replace('#','');
  if (h.length === 3) h = h[0]+h[0]+h[1]+h[1]+h[2]+h[2];
  var n = parseInt(h, 16);
  return [(n>>16)&255, (n>>8)&255, n&255].join(',');
}

function _dbRenderCatChart(data) {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('dbCatCanvas');
  if (!canvas) return;
  var W = canvas.clientWidth;
  if (!W || W < 10) { setTimeout(function(){ _dbRenderCatChart(data||window._dbData); }, 80); return; }

  // Net par catégorie hors épargne (dépenses − remboursements)
  var txSource = data.txFiltered.filter(function(t) {
    var amt = Number(t.amount) || 0;
    if (amt === 0) return false;
    if (data.savingsIds && data.savingsIds.has(String(t.category_id))) return false;
    var bkt = _dbGetBucketForCat(t.category_id) || 'Autre';
    return !/to assign|à assigner|a assigner|non assign/i.test(bkt)
        && String(t.category_id) !== 'CAT001';
  });
  if (_dbCrossFilter.type === 'bucket') {
    var bktF = _dbCrossFilter.value;
    txSource = txSource.filter(function(t) { return (_dbGetBucketForCat(t.category_id)||'Autre') === bktF; });
  }

  // Agréger par catégorie (net : positif = coûte, négatif = remboursé > dépensé)
  var byCat = {};
  txSource.forEach(function(t) {
    var n = _dbGetCatName(t);
    byCat[n] = (byCat[n] || 0) + Number(t.amount);
  });
  // N'afficher que les catégories avec coût net positif
  var items = Object.entries(byCat)
    .filter(function(e) { return e[1] > 0; })
    .sort(function(a,b){ return b[1]-a[1]; }).slice(0, window.innerWidth < 1024 ? 7 : 12);
  var maxVal = items.length ? items[0][1] : 1;

  // Hauteur dynamique — plus d'espace pour que les barres soient lisibles
  var isMobileCat = window.innerWidth < 1024;
  var rowH = isMobileCat ? 66 : 26, padT = 12, padB = 12;
  var fLabel = isMobileCat ? '20px' : '10px';
  var fAmt   = isMobileCat ? '17px' : '9px';
  var maxLabelChars = isMobileCat ? 12 : 13;
  var H = Math.max(160, items.length * rowH + padT + padB);
  // devicePixelRatio pour canvas net
  var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  canvas.style.width  = W + 'px';
  canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
  canvas.width  = W * dpr;
  canvas.height = H * dpr;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  var _prim2 = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--primary').trim() || '#6366F1';
  var palette   = [_prim2,'#8B5CF6','#EC4899','#EF4444','#F97316','#F59E0B','#10B981','#14B8A6','#3B82F6','#0EA5E9','#84CC16','#A3E635'];
  var textColor = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--text').trim() || '#fff';
  var textMuted = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#888';
  var activeFilter = _dbCrossFilter.type === 'category' ? _dbCrossFilter.value : null;

  if (!items.length) {
    ctx.fillStyle = textMuted;
    ctx.font = '12px system-ui';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText('Aucune dépense', W/2, H/2);
    canvas.onclick = null;
    return;
  }

  var labelW = isMobileCat ? Math.min(W*0.40, 148) : Math.min(W*0.36, 110);
  var amtW   = isMobileCat ? 78 : 52;
  var barAreaW = W - labelW - amtW - 4;
  var bh = rowH * 0.52;
  var barRects = [];

  items.forEach(function(item, i) {
    var catName = item[0], amt = item[1];
    var isSelected = activeFilter === catName;
    var alpha = !activeFilter || isSelected ? 1.0 : 0.28;
    var y = padT + i * rowH;
    var bw = maxVal > 0 ? (amt / maxVal) * barAreaW : 0;
    var barY = y + (rowH - bh) / 2;

    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
    // Label
    ctx.fillStyle = isSelected ? textColor : textMuted;
    ctx.font = (isSelected ? 'bold ' : '') + fLabel + ' system-ui';
    ctx.textAlign = 'right';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    var _cc = [...catName];
    var label = _cc.length > maxLabelChars ? _cc.slice(0, maxLabelChars-1).join('')+'…' : catName;
    ctx.fillText(label, labelW - 4, y + rowH/2);
    // Barre
    ctx.fillStyle = palette[i%palette.length];
    if (bw > 0) ctx.fillRect(labelW, barY, bw, bh);
    if (isSelected && bw > 0) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      ctx.strokeRect(labelW, barY, bw, bh);
    }
    // Montant
    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
    ctx.fillStyle = textMuted;
    ctx.font = fAmt + ' system-ui';
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.fillText(formatEuro(amt).replace(/[\s\u00a0]/g,''), labelW + bw + 4, y + rowH/2);

    barRects.push({ x:0, y: padT + i*rowH, w:W, h:rowH, name:catName, amount:amt, color:palette[i%palette.length] });
  });
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  // barRects sont en coordonnées CSS (post ctx.scale(dpr)), donc on mappe
  // la position souris sur l'espace CSS via rect.height (= H en CSS pixels)
  canvas.onclick = function(e) {
    var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var localY = (e.clientY - rect.top) * (H / rect.height);
    var hit = null;
    barRects.forEach(function(r) {
      if (localY >= r.y && localY < r.y + r.h) hit = r.name;
    });
    if (hit) _dbSetFilter('category', hit);
    else _dbClearFilter();
  };
  canvas.onmousemove = function(e) {
    var tip = document.getElementById('dbTooltip');
    if (!tip) return;
    var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var localY = (e.clientY - rect.top) * (H / rect.height);
    var hit = null;
    barRects.forEach(function(r) {
      if (localY >= r.y && localY < r.y + r.h) hit = r;
    });
    if (hit) {
      tip.innerHTML = '<span style="display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:2px;background:'+hit.color+';margin-right:5px;vertical-align:middle;"></span>'
        + '<strong>'+hit.name+'</strong><br>'
        + '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px;">'+formatEuro(hit.amount)+'</span>';
      tip.style.display = 'block';
      tip.style.left = (e.clientX + 14) + 'px';
      tip.style.top  = (e.clientY - 50) + 'px';
    } else { tip.style.display = 'none'; }
  };
  canvas.onmouseleave = function() {
    var tip = document.getElementById('dbTooltip');
    if (tip) tip.style.display = 'none';
  };
}

function _dbRenderBarChart() {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('dbBarCanvas');
  var legEl  = document.getElementById('dbBarLegend');
  if (!canvas) return;

  var W = canvas.clientWidth;
  if (!W || W < 10) { setTimeout(_dbRenderBarChart, 80); return; }
  var H = 200;
  canvas.width = W; canvas.height = H;

  var today = new Date();
  var months = [];
  for (var i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {
    var d = new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth()-i, 1);
    months.push(String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'/'+d.getFullYear());
  }

  var txAll = allTransactions || [];
  var monthData = months.map(function(p) {
    var txM = txAll.filter(function(t) { return _normalizePeriodKey(t.period) === p; });
    var rev = txM.filter(function(t) { return Number(t.amount) < 0; })
                 .reduce(function(s,t) { return s+Math.abs(Number(t.amount)); }, 0);
    var dep = txM.filter(function(t) { return Number(t.amount) > 0; })
                 .reduce(function(s,t) { return s+Number(t.amount); }, 0);
    return { p:p, rev:rev, dep:dep, net:rev-dep };
  });

  var maxVal = Math.max.apply(null, monthData.map(function(d) { return Math.max(d.rev, d.dep, 1); }));

  var padL=42, padR=8, padT=12, padB=28;
  var chartW = W-padL-padR;
  var chartH = H-padT-padB;
  var barW   = chartW/months.length;
  var groupW = barW*0.68;
  var bw     = groupW/2 - 1;

  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);

  var textColor = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#888';
  var segColor  = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--segment').trim()   || '#303138';

  // Grille horizontale (4 niveaux)
  for (var gi = 0; gi <= 4; gi++) {
    var gy = padT + chartH - (gi/4)*chartH;
    ctx.strokeStyle = segColor; ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(padL,gy); ctx.lineTo(W-padR,gy); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = textColor; ctx.font = '9px system-ui';
    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    var label = maxVal*gi/4 >= 1000
      ? Math.round(maxVal*gi/4/100)/10+'k'
      : Math.round(maxVal*gi/4)+'';
    ctx.fillText(label, padL-3, gy);
  }

  var netPoints = [];
  monthData.forEach(function(d, i) {
    var centerX = padL + i*barW + barW/2;
    var xR = centerX - groupW/2;
    var xD = centerX + 1;

    var hR = maxVal > 0 ? (d.rev/maxVal)*chartH : 0;
    var hD = maxVal > 0 ? (d.dep/maxVal)*chartH : 0;

    // Barre revenus
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(16,185,129,0.85)';
    if (hR > 0) ctx.fillRect(xR, padT+chartH-hR, bw, hR);

    // Barre dépenses
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(239,68,68,0.85)';
    if (hD > 0) ctx.fillRect(xD, padT+chartH-hD, bw, hD);

    // Label mois (MM/AA)
    ctx.fillStyle = textColor; ctx.font = '9px system-ui';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
    ctx.fillText(d.p.substring(0,2)+'/'+d.p.substring(5), centerX, H-padB+4);

    // Point net (centré sur la hauteur max — zéro = ligne médiane)
    var netY = padT + chartH - (Math.max(d.rev,d.dep) > 0 ? (Math.max(d.rev,d.dep)/maxVal)*chartH/2 + chartH/2 : chartH/2);
    // Simpler: net line relative to max bar
    var netH = maxVal > 0 ? ((d.rev-d.dep)/maxVal)*chartH/2 : 0;
    var netY2 = padT + chartH/2 - netH;
    netPoints.push({ x:centerX, y: Math.max(padT+2, Math.min(padT+chartH-2, netY2)) });
  });

  // Ligne zéro (centre du graphe net)
  var y0 = padT + chartH/2;
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.setLineDash([3,5]);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(padL,y0); ctx.lineTo(W-padR,y0); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  // Ligne net mensuel
  if (netPoints.length > 1) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#a78bfa'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.setLineDash([5,3]);
    ctx.beginPath();
    netPoints.forEach(function(pt,i) { if(i===0) ctx.moveTo(pt.x,pt.y); else ctx.lineTo(pt.x,pt.y); });
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);

    ctx.fillStyle = '#a78bfa';
    netPoints.forEach(function(pt) {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(pt.x,pt.y,3,0,2*Math.PI); ctx.fill();
    });
  }

  // Légende
  if (legEl) {
    legEl.innerHTML =
      '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;">' +
        '<span style="width:10px;height:10px;background:rgba(16,185,129,0.85);display:inline-block;border-radius:2px;"></span> Revenus</span> ' +
      '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;">' +
        '<span style="width:10px;height:10px;background:rgba(239,68,68,0.85);display:inline-block;border-radius:2px;"></span> Dépenses</span> ' +
      '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;">' +
        '<span style="width:14px;height:2px;background:#a78bfa;display:inline-block;border-radius:1px;border-top:2px dashed #a78bfa;height:0;"></span> Net mensuel</span>';
  }
}

// Synchronise le left de la selection-bar avec l'état du drawer
function _syncSelectionBar() {
  const bar = document.getElementById("selectionBar");
  if (!bar) return;
  // Même logique que .app :
  // mini drawer (toujours visible) = 70px
  // drawer épinglé = min(85vw, 340px)
  const pinned = document.body.classList.contains("drawer-pinned-mode");
  bar.style.left = pinned ? "min(85vw, 340px)" : "70px";
}

// ================= INIT =================
init();
function toggleDrawerState() {
      const b = document.body;
      const d = document.getElementById("sideDrawer");
      if (b.classList.contains("drawer-pinned-mode")) {
        togglePin();
        closeDrawer();
      } else if (d.classList.contains("open")) {
        closeDrawer();
      } else {
        openDrawer();
      }
    }

    function forceCloseDrawer() {
      if (document.body.classList.contains("drawer-pinned-mode")) togglePin();
      closeDrawer();
    }

    /* openDrawer / closeDrawer / togglePin → définitions globales plus haut */

function toggleProjectionDetail() {
  const detail = document.getElementById("projectionDetail");
  const icon   = document.getElementById("projToggleIcon");
  if (!detail) return;
  const open = detail.style.display !== "none";
  detail.style.display = open ? "none" : "flex";
  detail.style.flexDirection = "column";
  if (icon) icon.classList.toggle("open", !open);
}
