// ══════════════════════════════════════════
// ÉPARGNE
// ══════════════════════════════════════════
let _savingsDirty     = false;
let _sliderDraft      = {}; // id -> allocated (valeurs en cours de modification)

function showSavingsPage() {
  _hideOrderFloating();
  if (!_canNavigate()) return;
  hideAll();
  _setSheetScroll("locked");
  const sp = document.getElementById("savings-page");
  sp.style.display = "flex";
  setActiveNav("nav-savings");
  _setTitle("Épargne");
  document.getElementById("fabAdd").classList.remove("visible");
  // Si les données épargne ne sont pas encore chargées, recharger depuis GAS
  if (!allProjects.length && _totalSavings === 0) {
    showSpinner();
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(data => {
        hideSpinner();
        allProjects   = data.projects     || [];
        _totalSavings = data.totalSavings || 0;
        _savingsCatId = data.savingsCatId || _savingsCatId;
        _sliderDraft  = {};
        _renderSavings();
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        hideSpinner();
        showInfoModal("❌ " + (err.message || err));
      })
      .getSavingsData();
  } else {
    _renderSavings();
  }
}

function _renderSavings() {
  const total    = _totalSavings;
  const projects = allProjects;

  // Initialiser le draft avec les valeurs stockées
  projects.forEach(p => {
    if (_sliderDraft[p.id] === undefined) _sliderDraft[p.id] = p.allocated;
  });

  const sumAllocated = projects.reduce((s, p) => s + (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0), 0);
  const unalloc      = Math.max(0, total - sumAllocated);

  // Hero
  document.getElementById('savingsTotal').textContent   = formatEuro(total);
  const _unallocWrap = document.getElementById('savingsUnallocWrap');
  const _unallocEl   = document.getElementById('savingsUnalloc');
  if (_unallocEl)  _unallocEl.textContent = unalloc > 0.01 ? formatEuro(unalloc) : '';
  if (_unallocWrap) {
    _unallocWrap.style.display = unalloc > 0.01 ? '' : 'none';
    _unallocWrap.classList.toggle('has-unalloc', unalloc > 0.01);
  }
  const btnAssign = document.getElementById('btnAssignRest');
  if (btnAssign) btnAssign.style.display = (unalloc > 0.01 && projects.length) ? '' : 'none';

  const list  = document.getElementById('savingsProjectList');
  const empty = document.getElementById('savingsEmpty');
  list.innerHTML = '';

  if (!projects.length) {
    empty.style.display = '';
    return;
  }
  empty.style.display = 'none';

  projects.forEach(p => {
    const alloc  = _sliderDraft[p.id] || 0;
    const pct    = total > 0 ? Math.round(alloc / total * 100) : 0;
    const tgtPct = p.target > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round(alloc / p.target * 100)) : null;

    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'savings-project-card' + (alloc !== p.allocated ? ' dirty' : '');
    card.id = 'proj-card-' + p.id;

    card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="savings-project-top">
        <div style="flex:1;">
          <div class="savings-project-name">${p.name}</div>
          ${p.target > 0 ? `<div class="savings-project-target" id="proj-tgt-label-${p.id}">${tgtPct}% de l'objectif ${formatEuro(p.target)}</div>` : ''}
        </div>
        <div class="savings-project-alloc-wrap">
          <input type="number" class="savings-amount-input" id="proj-alloc-${p.id}"
            value="${alloc.toFixed(2)}" min="0" max="${total.toFixed(2)}" step="0.01">
          <span class="savings-currency">€</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${p.target > 0 ? `
        <div class="savings-target-bar">
          <div class="savings-target-fill" id="proj-tgt-${p.id}" style="width:${tgtPct}%;"></div>
        </div>` : ''}
      <div class="savings-slider-row">
        <input type="range" class="savings-slider" id="slider-${p.id}"
          min="0" max="${Math.round(total * 100)}" step="1"
          value="${Math.round(alloc * 100)}">
        <span class="savings-slider-pct" id="proj-pct-${p.id}">${pct}%</span>
      </div>
      <div class="savings-project-footer">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;">
          <button class="btn-savings-edit" onclick="_openEditProject('${p.id}')">✏️ Modifier</button>
          <button class="btn-savings-delete" onclick="_deleteProject('${p.id}')">🗑 Supprimer</button>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;">
          <button class="btn-savings-withdraw" onclick="_openWithdrawModal('${p.id}')">💸 Utiliser</button>
          <button class="btn-assign-unalloc" id="btn-assign-${p.id}" onclick="_assignUnallocTo('${p.id}')" style="display:none;">
            ＋ Ajouter
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    // Slider : logique zéro-sum
    const slider = card.querySelector('.savings-slider');
    slider.addEventListener('input', () => {
      _onSliderChange(p.id, Number(slider.value) / 100);
    });

    // Input montant : déclenche le même zéro-sum
    const amtInput = card.querySelector('.savings-amount-input');
    if (amtInput && window.innerWidth < 1024) {
      amtInput.style.setProperty('font-size', '34px', 'important');
      amtInput.style.setProperty('width', '160px', 'important');
      amtInput.style.setProperty('min-height', '64px', 'important');
    }
    amtInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
      const raw       = parseFloat(amtInput.value) || 0;
      const oldVal    = _sliderDraft[p.id] || 0;
      const sumOthers = allProjects.filter(px => px.id !== p.id)
                                   .reduce((s, px) => s + (_sliderDraft[px.id] || 0), 0);
      const maxVal    = Math.max(0, _totalSavings - sumOthers);
      const v         = Math.max(0, Math.min(raw, maxVal));
      _onSliderChange(p.id, v);
    });
    amtInput.addEventListener('blur', () => {
      // Recaler la valeur affichée sur le draft réel
      const real = _sliderDraft[p.id] || 0;
      amtInput.value = real.toFixed(2);
    });

    list.appendChild(card);
  });

  // Save bar
  const saveBar = document.getElementById('savingsSaveBar');
  const hasDirty = projects.some(p => Math.abs((_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0) - p.allocated) > 0.005);
  saveBar.style.display = hasDirty ? '' : 'none';
}

function _onSliderChange(movedId, newValue) {
  const total    = _totalSavings;
  const projects = allProjects;

  const oldValue  = _sliderDraft[movedId] || 0;
  const sumOthers = projects.filter(p => p.id !== movedId)
                            .reduce((s, p) => s + (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0), 0);
  // Max réel = ce qui n'est pas encore pris par les autres
  const maxAllowed = Math.max(0, total - sumOthers);
  newValue = Math.max(0, Math.min(newValue, maxAllowed));

  if (Math.abs(newValue - oldValue) < 0.005) return;

  // Simple : on change ce projet, le reste va dans non-réparti
  // Jamais de redistribution forcée sur les autres
  _sliderDraft[movedId] = newValue;
  _updateSavingsUI();
}

// Assigner tout le non-réparti à un projet spécifique
function _assignUnallocTo(projectId) {
  const projects   = allProjects;
  const sumAlloc   = projects.reduce((s, p) => s + (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0), 0);
  const unalloc    = Math.max(0, _totalSavings - sumAlloc);
  if (unalloc < 0.01) return;
  _sliderDraft[projectId] = (_sliderDraft[projectId] || 0) + unalloc;
  _updateSavingsUI();
}

function _updateSavingsUI() {
  const total    = _totalSavings;
  const projects = allProjects;
  const sumAllocated = projects.reduce((s, p) => s + (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0), 0);
  const unalloc  = Math.max(0, total - sumAllocated);

  const _unallocWrap = document.getElementById('savingsUnallocWrap');
  const _unallocEl   = document.getElementById('savingsUnalloc');
  if (_unallocEl)  _unallocEl.textContent = unalloc > 0.01 ? formatEuro(unalloc) : '';
  if (_unallocWrap) {
    _unallocWrap.style.display = unalloc > 0.01 ? '' : 'none';
    _unallocWrap.classList.toggle('has-unalloc', unalloc > 0.01);
  }
  const _btnA = document.getElementById('btnAssignRest');
  if (_btnA) _btnA.style.display = (unalloc > 0.01 && projects.length) ? '' : 'none';

  projects.forEach(p => {
    const alloc = _sliderDraft[p.id] || 0;
    const pct   = total > 0 ? Math.round(alloc / total * 100) : 0;

    const allocEl  = document.getElementById('proj-alloc-' + p.id);
    const pctEl    = document.getElementById('proj-pct-'   + p.id);
    const sliderEl = document.getElementById('slider-'     + p.id);
    const tgtEl    = document.getElementById('proj-tgt-'   + p.id);
    const cardEl   = document.getElementById('proj-card-'  + p.id);

    if (allocEl && allocEl.tagName === 'INPUT') {
      // Ne pas interrompre une saisie en cours
      if (document.activeElement !== allocEl) allocEl.value = alloc.toFixed(2);
    } else if (allocEl) {
      allocEl.textContent = formatEuro(alloc);
    }
    if (pctEl)    pctEl.textContent    = pct + '%';
    if (sliderEl) sliderEl.value = Math.round(alloc * 100);
    if (tgtEl && p.target > 0) {
      const tPct = Math.min(100, Math.round(alloc / p.target * 100));
      tgtEl.style.width = tPct + '%';
    }
    if (cardEl) {
      const dirty = Math.abs(alloc - p.allocated) > 0.005;
      cardEl.classList.toggle('dirty', dirty);
    }
    const assignBtn = document.getElementById('btn-assign-' + p.id);
    if (assignBtn) {
      const sumAlloc = projects.reduce((s, px) => s + (_sliderDraft[px.id] || 0), 0);
      const unalloc  = _totalSavings - sumAlloc;
      assignBtn.style.display = unalloc > 0.01 ? '' : 'none';
      assignBtn.textContent   = '＋ ' + formatEuro(unalloc);
    }
  });

  const hasDirty = projects.some(p => Math.abs((_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0) - p.allocated) > 0.005);
  document.getElementById('savingsSaveBar').style.display = hasDirty ? '' : 'none';
}

function _assignRemainder() {
  const total    = _totalSavings;
  const projects = allProjects;
  if (!projects.length) return;
  const sumAlloc = projects.reduce((s, p) => s + (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0), 0);
  const unalloc  = Math.max(0, total - sumAlloc);
  if (unalloc < 0.01) return;

  // Répartir proportionnellement aux allocations existantes
  // Si tout est à 0, répartir équitablement
  const sumDraft = projects.reduce((s, p) => s + (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0), 0);
  if (sumDraft < 0.01) {
    // Répartition égale
    const share = unalloc / projects.length;
    projects.forEach(p => { _sliderDraft[p.id] = (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0) + share; });
  } else {
    // Proportionnel aux parts existantes
    projects.forEach(p => {
      const ratio = (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0) / sumDraft;
      _sliderDraft[p.id] = (_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0) + unalloc * ratio;
    });
  }
  _updateSavingsUI();
}

function _saveSavingsAllocations() {
  const allocations = allProjects.map(p => ({
    id:        p.id,
    allocated: Math.round((_sliderDraft[p.id] || 0) * 100) / 100
  }));
  showSpinner();
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(projects => {
      hideSpinner();
      allProjects = projects || allProjects;
      _sliderDraft = {};
      _renderSavings();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
      hideSpinner();
      showInfoModal('❌ ' + (err.message || err));
    })
    .saveProjectAllocations(allocations);
}

function openAddProjectModal() {
  document.getElementById('addProjectName').value   = '';
  document.getElementById('addProjectTarget').value = '';
  document.getElementById('addProjectModal').classList.add('active');
  setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('addProjectName').focus(), 150);
}
function closeAddProjectModal() {
  document.getElementById('addProjectModal').classList.remove('active');
}
function _confirmAddProject() {
  const name   = document.getElementById('addProjectName').value.trim();
  const target = parseFloat(document.getElementById('addProjectTarget').value) || 0;
  if (!name) { document.getElementById('addProjectName').focus(); return; }
  closeAddProjectModal();
  showSpinner();
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(projects => {
      hideSpinner();
      allProjects  = projects || allProjects;
      _sliderDraft = {};
      _renderSavings();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => { hideSpinner(); showInfoModal('❌ ' + (err.message || err)); })
    .addProject(name, target);
}

function _openWithdrawModal(id) {
  const p = allProjects.find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!p) return;
  const balance = Math.max(0, (p.allocated || 0) - (p.withdrawn || 0));
  // CONTRÔLE : rien à retirer → pas de retrait possible
  if (balance <= 0) {
    showInfoModal("💸 Retrait impossible : le disponible de <b>" + _esc(p.name) + "</b> est à 0 €.<br><small>Allouez d'abord de l'épargne à ce projet.</small>");
    return;
  }
  // Compte destinataire du retrait (multi-comptes : principal présélectionné)
  const wSel = document.getElementById('withdrawAccount');
  if (wSel) {
    wSel.innerHTML = "";
    (allAccounts || []).filter(a => a.active !== false).forEach(a => {
      const opt = document.createElement("option");
      opt.value = a.id;
      opt.textContent = (a.is_default ? "⭐ " : "🏦 ") + a.name;
      if (a.is_default) opt.selected = true;
      wSel.appendChild(opt);
    });
  }
  document.getElementById('withdrawProjectId').value      = id;
  document.getElementById('withdrawProjectName').textContent  = p.name;
  document.getElementById('withdrawProjectBalance').textContent = formatEuro(balance);
  document.getElementById('withdrawAmount').value   = '';
  document.getElementById('withdrawAmount').max     = balance.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('withdrawDesc').value     = '';
  document.getElementById('withdrawDate').value     = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  document.getElementById('withdrawModal').classList.add('active');
  setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('withdrawAmount').focus(), 150);
}
function closeWithdrawModal() {
  document.getElementById('withdrawModal').classList.remove('active');
}
function _confirmWithdraw() {
  const id     = document.getElementById('withdrawProjectId').value;
  const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('withdrawAmount').value);
  const desc   = document.getElementById('withdrawDesc').value.trim() || 'Retrait épargne';
  const date   = document.getElementById('withdrawDate').value;
  const p      = allProjects.find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!p || !amount || amount <= 0) {
    document.getElementById('withdrawAmount').focus(); return;
  }
  const balance = Math.max(0, (p.allocated || 0) - (p.withdrawn || 0));
  if (amount > balance + 0.01) {
    document.getElementById('withdrawAmount').value = balance.toFixed(2); return;
  }
  closeWithdrawModal();
  showSpinner();
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(data => {
      if (!data) { hideSpinner(); showInfoModal('❌ Erreur : réponse vide du serveur. Rechargez l\'app.'); return; }
      // Mettre à jour project.allocated : on soustrait le retrait du projet
      if (data.projects) {
        allProjects = data.projects;
      } else {
        // Fallback : réduire allocated du projet retiré
        const proj = allProjects.find(x => x.id === id);
        if (proj) proj.allocated = Math.max(0, (proj.allocated || 0) - amount);
      }
      _totalSavings = data.totalSavings || 0;
      _sliderDraft  = {};
      allProjects.forEach(pr => { _sliderDraft[pr.id] = pr.allocated || 0; });
      _renderSavings();
      // Mettre à jour les transactions + projection + budget
      if (data.transactions && data.transactions.length >= 0) {
        afterMutation(data.transactions, 'Retrait épargne');
      } else {
        hideSpinner();
      }
      const msg = "💸 " + formatEuro(amount) + " retirés de \"" + p.name + "\"\nLe reste à dépenser de votre catégorie épargne a augmenté d'autant.\nDistribuez-le depuis l'onglet Budget.";
      showInfoModal(msg);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => { hideSpinner(); showInfoModal('❌ ' + (err.message || err)); })
    .withdrawFromProject(id, amount, desc, date, (document.getElementById('withdrawAccount') || {}).value || "");
}

function _openEditProject(id) {
  const p = allProjects.find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!p) return;
  document.getElementById('editProjectId').value    = id;
  document.getElementById('editProjectName').value  = p.name;
  document.getElementById('editProjectTarget').value = p.target > 0 ? p.target : '';
  document.getElementById('editProjectModal').classList.add('active');
  setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('editProjectName').focus(), 150);
}
function closeEditProjectModal() {
  document.getElementById('editProjectModal').classList.remove('active');
}
function _confirmEditProject() {
  const id     = document.getElementById('editProjectId').value;
  const name   = document.getElementById('editProjectName').value.trim();
  const target = parseFloat(document.getElementById('editProjectTarget').value) || 0;
  if (!name) { document.getElementById('editProjectName').focus(); return; }
  closeEditProjectModal();
  showSpinner();
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(projects => {
      hideSpinner();
      allProjects  = projects || allProjects;
      _sliderDraft = {};
      _renderSavings();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => { hideSpinner(); showInfoModal('❌ ' + (err.message || err)); })
    .updateProject(id, name, target);
}

function _deleteProject(id) {
  const p = allProjects.find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!p) return;
  const alloc = _sliderDraft[id] || p.allocated || 0;
  document.getElementById('deleteProjectName').textContent = p.name;
  const allocEl = document.getElementById('deleteProjectAlloc');
  allocEl.textContent = alloc > 0.01 ? formatEuro(alloc) + ' retourneront dans le non-réparti.' : 'Aucun montant alloué.';
  document.getElementById('deleteProjectModal').classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('deleteProjectModal').dataset.pendingId = id;
}
function closeDeleteProjectModal() {
  document.getElementById('deleteProjectModal').classList.remove('active');
}
function _confirmDeleteProject() {
  const id = document.getElementById('deleteProjectModal').dataset.pendingId;
  closeDeleteProjectModal();
  showSpinner();
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(projects => {
      hideSpinner();
      allProjects  = projects || allProjects;
      delete _sliderDraft[id];
      _renderSavings();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => { hideSpinner(); showInfoModal('❌ ' + (err.message || err)); })
    .deleteProject(id);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ANIMATION SPLASH SCREEN
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
(function() {
  const _wait = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
  let _auraRAF = null;

  function _startAuraPulse(el) {
    const start = performance.now();
    const FADE=900, PERIOD=3200, SMIN=0.95, SMAX=1.35;
    const tick = now => {
      const e = now - start;
      const fade = Math.min(e / FADE, 1);
      const sin  = (Math.sin((e / PERIOD) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2) + 1) / 2;
      const sc   = SMIN + sin * (SMAX - SMIN);
      el.style.transform = `scale(${(fade < 1 ? sc * fade : sc).toFixed(4)})`;
      el.style.opacity   = (fade * 0.92).toFixed(4);
      _auraRAF = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    _auraRAF = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  function _stopAuraPulse() { if (_auraRAF) { cancelAnimationFrame(_auraRAF); _auraRAF = null; } }

  function _spawnParticles(canvas) {
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const W = canvas.width = 400, H = canvas.height = 400;
    const cx = W/2, cy = H/2;
    const particles = Array.from({length: 28}, () => {
      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const speed = 1.8 + Math.random() * 3.2;
      return { x:cx, y:cy, vx:Math.cos(angle)*speed, vy:Math.sin(angle)*speed-1.5,
               r:2.5+Math.random()*3.5, alpha:1, gravity:0.07+Math.random()*0.05,
               color: Math.random()>0.4 ? '#FBD72F' : '#FFE040' };
    });
    const tick = () => {
      ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
      let alive = false;
      particles.forEach(p => {
        p.x+=p.vx; p.y+=p.vy; p.vy+=p.gravity; p.alpha-=0.010;
        if (p.alpha > 0) {
          alive = true;
          ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;
          ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,p.r,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
        }
      });
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      if (alive) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function _shineSweep() {
    const shine = document.getElementById('shine');
    if (!shine) return;
    shine.style.opacity='1';
    shine.style.transition='left 0.85s ease-in-out';
    shine.style.left='140%';
    setTimeout(() => { shine.style.opacity='0'; }, 850);
  }

  let _cursorInt = null;
  function _startCursor(el) { let on=true; el.style.opacity='1'; _cursorInt=setInterval(()=>{ on=!on; el.style.opacity=on?'1':'0'; },550); }
  function _stopCursor(el)  { clearInterval(_cursorInt); el.style.opacity='0'; }

  // ── Fermer les modals en cliquant en dehors ────────────────
  document.addEventListener("click", function(e) {
    if (!e.target.classList.contains("modal-overlay")) return;
    if (!e.target.classList.contains("active")) return;
    const cancelBtn = e.target.querySelector(".btn-cancel");
    if (cancelBtn) { cancelBtn.click(); return; }
    e.target.classList.remove("active");
  });

  async function _runSplashAnimation() {
    const logo    = document.getElementById('logo-wrap');
    const aura    = document.getElementById('aura');
    const shock   = document.getElementById('shockwave');
    const textBlk = document.getElementById('text-block');
    const typed   = document.getElementById('typed');
    const cursor  = document.getElementById('cursor');
    const line    = document.getElementById('tagline-line');
    const pCanvas = document.getElementById('particle-canvas');
    const q       = document.getElementById('wimm-q');
    const shine   = document.getElementById('shine');
    if (!logo || !aura || !shock || !textBlk) return;

    const TAGLINE = "Where is my money?";

    // Reset
    _stopAuraPulse(); _stopCursor(cursor);
    logo.style.transition='none'; logo.style.opacity='0'; logo.style.transform='translateY(-340px) scale(1)';
    aura.style.transition='none'; aura.style.opacity='0'; aura.style.transform='scale(0)';
    shock.style.transition='none'; shock.style.opacity='0'; shock.style.transform='scale(0.1)';
    textBlk.style.transition='none'; textBlk.style.opacity='0';
    if (typed)  typed.textContent='';
    if (line)  { line.style.transition='none'; line.style.width='0'; }
    if (shine) { shine.style.transition='none'; shine.style.left='-80%'; shine.style.opacity='0'; }
    if (q)     { q.style.transition='none'; q.style.opacity='0'; q.style.transform='scale(0) rotate(-20deg)'; }
    if (pCanvas) pCanvas.getContext('2d').clearRect(0,0,400,400);
    void document.body.offsetHeight;
    await new Promise(r => requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(r)));
    await _wait(300);

    // Phase 1 : Chute
    logo.style.transition='opacity 0.05s linear, transform 0.42s cubic-bezier(0.4,0,1,1)';
    logo.style.opacity='1'; logo.style.transform='translateY(16px) scale(1)';
    await _wait(420);

    // Phase 2 : Impact
    logo.style.transition='transform 0.07s ease-out'; logo.style.transform='translateY(16px) scale(1.05,0.95)';
    shock.style.transition='transform 0.65s ease-out, opacity 0.65s ease-out';
    shock.style.transform='scale(3)'; shock.style.opacity='0';
    if (pCanvas) _spawnParticles(pCanvas);
    await _wait(70);

    // Phase 3 : Rebonds
    logo.style.transition='transform 0.20s ease-out'; logo.style.transform='translateY(-24px) scale(1)'; await _wait(200);
    logo.style.transition='transform 0.14s ease-in';  logo.style.transform='translateY(10px) scale(1.02,0.98)'; await _wait(140);
    logo.style.transition='transform 0.12s ease-out'; logo.style.transform='translateY(-8px) scale(1)'; await _wait(120);
    logo.style.transition='transform 0.10s ease-in';  logo.style.transform='translateY(3px) scale(1.01)'; await _wait(100);
    logo.style.transition='transform 0.12s ease-in-out'; logo.style.transform='translateY(0) scale(1)';
    await _wait(180);

    // Phase 4+5 fusionnées : shine + aura + WIMM? simultanés (v2)
    // Le shine balaye pendant que WIMM? apparaît — aucune attente entre les deux
    _shineSweep();                                      // 850ms, non-bloquant
    _startAuraPulse(aura);
    textBlk.style.transition='opacity 750ms ease';     // 750ms vs 1600ms avant
    textBlk.style.opacity='1';

    // Phase 6 : Frappe tagline — démarre à t+500ms (WIMM à ~65% visible)
    await _wait(500);
    if (typed && cursor) {
      _startCursor(cursor);
      let i = 0;
      await new Promise(resolve => {
        const type = () => {
          if (i < TAGLINE.length) { typed.textContent += TAGLINE[i++]; setTimeout(type, 72 + Math.random()*40); }
          else resolve();
        };
        type();
      });
      await _wait(200);
    }

    // Phase 7 : ? projeté
    if (q) {
      q.style.transition='none'; q.style.opacity='1';
      q.style.transform='scale(3.5) translateY(-60px) rotate(-25deg)';
      void q.offsetHeight;
      q.style.transition='transform 0.12s cubic-bezier(0.6,0,1,1)';
      q.style.transform='scale(1.6) translateY(4px) rotate(10deg)';
      await _wait(90);
      q.style.transition='transform 0.07s ease-out'; q.style.transform='scale(0.85) translateY(-3px) rotate(-5deg)'; await _wait(70);
      q.style.transition='transform 0.06s ease-in-out'; q.style.transform='scale(1.05) translateY(1px) rotate(2deg)'; await _wait(60);
      q.style.transition='transform 0.08s ease-out'; q.style.transform='scale(1) translateY(0) rotate(0deg)';
    }
    if (line) { await _wait(400); line.style.width='180px'; }
  }

  // Lancer dès que le DOM est prêt
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', _runSplashAnimation);
  } else {
    _runSplashAnimation();
  }
})();
</script>
